From  the  Receipts  we  oblerve  :

1.  That  Metals  and  Minerals  volatilized  with  Simple  Mi -

Vinegar.

of  Things  tinging  Aiffolved  in  Simple  Mineral  Menftruums.

the  Eighth  Kind  ,  becaufe  fronger.

5.  That  the  diffolutions  of  Metals  performed  by  thefe  Mercu¬
rial  Menftruums ,  have  been  by  the  Adepts  fometimes  called  A-
malgamations.  You  muft  know  ,  faith  Ifaacus ,  That  this
is  the  beft  Solution  ,  that  ever  was  found  in  the  World ,  for
herein  is  no  error  of  Proportion  and  Weight.  For  Nature
errs  not.  For  when  Mercury  is  diftblved ,  it  diflolves  other
Metals  alfo,  as  is  rightly  taught  in  other  places^  Nor  will  it
